103:1. **書き込み境界（前提 6、ランタイム別に正直に）** Claude worker 席は専用の設定ディレクトリ `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR=~/.claude-worker`（chezmoi が描画、operator が一度 login）で起動し、orchestrator の `~/.claude` と設定を共有しない。worker 設定：除外コマンド無し、Herdr socket 無し、sandbox の allowWrite は自席 worktree と agmsg store と席別キャッシュだけ、`failIfUnavailable: true`、信頼パス（anchored checkout の `.claude/worktrees/` 以外、`~/.agents`、`~/.claude*`、`~/.local`、`~/.config`）への Edit deny（公式文書：file tool の permission 検査は要求パスと symlink 解決後のパスの両方に deny を適用し、symlink そのものへの書き込みは拒否する）、sandbox の強制範囲はユーザー設定ではなく **managed settings**（`/Library/Application Support/ClaudeCode/managed-settings.json`、公式文書 "Repository settings under an admin-required sandbox"：project/local 設定は sandbox を無効化したり除外・socket を足したりできない）に置く。chezmoi は user ではこのファイルを書けないので、描画した内容を操作者が `sudo` で一度配置する（操作者手順）。worker 設定は worktree 自身の `.claude/**` と `.mcp.json` への Edit deny（`//<anchored>/.claude/worktrees/*/.claude/**`）を持つ（Bash 側は sandbox 既定の settings/hooks の denyWithinAllow）。agmsg store（全席が書く）から配送 hook が driver や plugin を source する上流の経路（storage.sh の `drivers.partition` traversal、`db/trusted-plugins` と `plugins/partition/shared.sh` による差し替え承認）は別タスク（agmsg hardening）の行で、worker 設定は `~/.agents/skills/agmsg/plugins/**` と `~/.agents/skills/agmsg/db/trusted-plugins` への書き込みを deny し（Edit deny は sandbox の deny にも合流する）、hook 側は store 由来の設定値を検証するまで残余と書く。managed settings の配置先は OS で異なる（macOS `/Library/Application Support/ClaudeCode/managed-settings.json`、Linux `/etc/claude-code/managed-settings.json`）。hook は agmsg 配送だけを、hook コマンドを `/usr/bin/env -i PATH=/usr/bin:/bin HOME=<home> …` で始めて（`BASH_ENV` などの継承環境を bash 起動前に消す；PATH 固定だけでは BASH_ENV が先に走る）絶対パスのインタプリタで呼び（上流の agmsg スクリプトは `/usr/bin/env bash` の shebang を持つので、hook 実行環境の PATH が shim を含まないことで束縛する）、各 hook に `onFailure: "block"`。公式文書：linked worktree の session は **main checkout の `.claude/settings.local.json` を読む**ので、worker 向けの hook を worktree の settings.local.json に書いても読まれず、逆に orchestrator の local hooks が worker session でも走る。したがって orchestrator 側の local hooks も guard 経由にし、worker 側の hook は worker config dir の settings に置く。Edit deny の一覧は `git ls-tree` ではなく描画時の静的一覧（manifest に列挙、`.agents/worklog/**` と `.agents/worklog/uv-cache/**` を含む）とし、`~/.agents/**` の全面 deny は agmsg store の書き込みと矛盾するので、`~/.agents/agent-config.yaml`、`~/.agents/skills/**/scripts/**`、`~/.agents/model-profiles.env` などストア以外を列挙する。公式文書で確認した事実（2026-10-19）：`Edit(path)` の deny は Write/MultiEdit/NotebookEdit にも効き、deny → ask → allow の順で最初の一致が決まる；ただし Read/Edit の deny は **Bash sandbox の設定にも合流する**（"Paths and domains from both sandbox settings and permission rules are merged into the final sandbox configuration"）ので、ユーザー設定（全席共有）に `.orchestration/**` の Edit deny を書くと orchestrator の Bash による記録書き込みも止まる。hook は起動時に固定されず、設定ファイルの編集は file watcher が即座に反映する。`sandbox.failIfUnavailable` の既定は fail-open（依存が無ければ sandbox 無しで実行）。したがって Claude 席の境界は Edit deny 規則ではなく、**ユーザーレベルの PreToolUse hook**（`~/.claude/hooks/worker-write-boundary`、chezmoi が描画、`/bin/sh` または絶対パスの `python3 -I`）で作る：cwd の realpath が `<anchored>/.claude/worktrees/` 配下の session では、Write/Edit/MultiEdit/NotebookEdit の対象が cwd の外、または `<cwd>/.claude/**`・`<cwd>/.mcp.json` のとき `permissionDecision: deny` を返す。Bash 側は sandbox の既定 `denyWithinAllow`（settings、hooks）と `allowWrite`（自席 worktree）が担う。`failIfUnavailable: true` を全席に置く。 Codex 席は sandbox（workspace-write）がツールの書き込み全部を自席の worktree と列挙パスに限る。Claude 席は Seatbelt sandbox が Bash を限るだけで、Write/Edit/MultiEdit/NotebookEdit、hook、MCP プロセスは sandbox 外で動き、permission policy（permgate の決定論ポリシー）が path で拒否しない限り anchored checkout や `~/.agents` にも書ける（2026-10-19 時点、描画済み設定にその deny は無い）。したがって前提 6 は「Codex 席は成立、Claude 席は P1-15 が描画設定に Edit の path deny を入れるまで残余」と書く。P1-15 以前に「worker は信頼ルートに書けない」と書く文は誤りで、C-80 が拒否する。P1-15 が入るまで、承認の出所は項目 8 の「コミット済み台帳」で守る。書き込みは自席の worktree（`.claude/worktrees/<name>`）、scratch、列挙されたツールキャッシュに限る。anchored checkout（`.claude/worktrees/` 以外）、`~/.agents/agent-config.yaml`、`~/.claude/hooks`、信頼ルートのバイナリ、orchestrator の証跡・スナップショット・lock は worker から書けない。render テストが描画後の deny を検証する。残余は「どの席 sandbox にも属さない同一 UID プロセス」と、Worker Playbook step 4 が sandbox 外に出す少数のコマンド（gh）。後者は D-024-17（P1-15）で絶対パスの実行体に束縛する。
104:2. **無害化した環境を最初に** 最初の処理として `env -i` に入り、PATH は信頼ルートで解決した絶対バイナリへのリンクだけの私設ディレクトリ（shims、空、相対は拒否）。git は毎回 `GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM=1 GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL=/dev/null -c core.hooksPath=/dev/null -c core.fsmonitor=false -c protocol.allow=never -c protocol.https.allow=always` と明示 `-C`（https だけ許す。`protocol.allow=never` 単独は必要な fetch を止める）。 検査の読み取りは `GIT_NO_LAZY_FETCH=1` で部分クローンの遅延 fetch（checkout 設定の credential.helper を起動しうる）を止め、`-c submodule.recurse=false --ignore-submodules=all` で submodule 内の status/diff（submodule 自身の設定が filter を有効化しうる）を止める。`.gitmodules` を持つ checkout は検査対象として拒否する。 `-C` は作業木を束縛しないので（repo-local の `core.worktree` が読み先を checkout 外へ向けられる）、git は `--git-dir=<checkout>/.git --work-tree=<checkout>` を明示し、`git rev-parse --show-toplevel` の realpath が checkout と一致しないときは拒否する。 premise や検査の「コマンド実行」は、固定語彙の読み取り専用プローブ（`git rev-parse <ref>`、`test -e <path>`、`grep -c <pattern> <file>` のように検査器自身が解釈する形）だけを許し、任意のシェル文字列は実行しない（本文のハッシュはコマンド文を認証するだけで、`python -I scripts/x.py` のファイル被演算子や `source ./x.sh` の中身は認証しない）。diff と show は `--no-ext-diff --no-textconv` と `-c core.attributesFile=/dev/null -c core.sshCommand=false` を付け、PR 側の `.gitattributes`（external diff、textconv、filter）が検査内容や実行物を変えないようにする。Python は `-I`。PATH 探索で実行するものは無い。 A shell script that runs under a PR-controlled or worker-controlled environment (verdict.sh, the chain glue, CI steps) is executed as a program with `#!/bin/bash -p`, never as `bash <file>`, and only by a trusted parent that supplies the clean environment before the exec: `/usr/bin/env -i PATH=/usr/bin:/bin HOME=<passwd home> LANG=C.UTF-8 /bin/bash --noprofile --norc -p <absolute script path> …` (long options before `-p`). Privileged mode discards inherited functions, `BASH_ENV`/`ENV`, `SHELLOPTS`, `BASHOPTS`, `CDPATH` and `GLOBIGNORE`, but on Linux an inherited `LD_PRELOAD`/`LD_AUDIT` already executes while the loader maps `/bin/bash`, before `-p` or any statement runs, so a self re-exec inside the script is not a boundary and re-executing `"$0"` would reopen the caller's pathname (a worker-owned symlink can be swapped between the two executions); the program therefore verifies and refuses instead of repairing: its environment must consist of exactly the allowed names (checked through absolute paths `/usr/bin/env`, `/usr/bin/grep`), `$0` resolved with `realpath` must be a regular file under the repository root, and HOME must equal the passwd home of the real uid (`/usr/bin/dscl . -read /Users/<user> NFSHomeDirectory` on macOS, `/usr/bin/getent passwd <uid>` on Linux), otherwise exit 2 with a named message. A caller-set sentinel variable is not a sanitization proof, because the caller controls it (ci-efficiency a04 and verdict a04 C-80 reviews, 2026-10-19). No script accepts a caller-supplied executable directory; interpreters are the fixed absolute paths item 9 names.
105:3. **信頼パスの境界検査** 仕組みが信頼するパス（スクリプト、台帳、証跡、worktree、lock、出力先）は realpath で解決し、`.claude/worktrees/` または manifest の worker ルート配下なら拒否（ask/hold）。symlink 構成要素は解決後に比較し、大小文字を区別しないファイルシステムでは同一性比較を大小文字非依存で行う。 A validator or scanner enumerates its inputs by walking the filesystem under fixed roots (every regular file, ignored or untracked included), never by asking git which files exist: `git ls-files` trusts the index and the working-tree `.gitignore` rules, which a same-UID writer edits without a commit (a07 C-80 rounds 1 to 3, 2026-10-19); git is consulted only to confirm the root (`--show-toplevel` equality with a real `.git` directory) and never as an exclusion authority.
106:4. **検証したものを実行し、検証した内容を渡す** 検証した実行体はその realpath で実行する（再探索しない）。検証したファイル内容は信頼ルート配下のスナップショット（0600、一意名）として渡し、使用直前に sha256 を再照合する。判断に読んだ証跡は sha256 を出力に記録する。
109:0. **脅威モデル（D-024-21）** 席は同じ OS ユーザで動き、ホストは席間の信頼境界ではない（同一 UID のプロセスは symlink 差し替え、共有 `.git` の object 差し替え、hook の子プロセス、Herdr socket 経由の host 実行で、ホスト内のどの検査も迂回できる）。regime の硬い境界は GitHub（保護された main、必須チェック、orchestrator の identity によるマージ）。ただしこの境界は **worker 席が orchestrator の GitHub 資格情報を共有しない**ときだけ成立する（D-024-22：worker 席は専用の GitHub identity（別アカウントまたは App のトークン）で `gh` に login し、ruleset の bypass 一覧に入らない。orchestrator の keychain トークンを worker の config dir に持ち込まない。identity が分かれるまで「GitHub が境界」は未成立と書く）。**同一 macOS ユーザでは資格情報は分離できない**（gh は keychain の項目を host/user で引き、同じ UID の gh は orchestrator のトークンを読める。`GH_CONFIG_DIR` は設定を分けるだけ）。防止には worker 席を別の OS ユーザ（または別ホスト）で動かすしかなく、それは D-024-23 の将来項目（P1-16、操作者の判断）。それまで GitHub 境界は **検知**で守る：記録は boundary の記録 PR まで main に無いので、検知は次の記録 PR の regime ジョブで行う（`.orchestration/acceptance/**` に触れる PR で、前回 boundary 以降の main の各マージ commit に、gate 通過の証跡を持つ受け入れ記録が対応することを検査し、無ければその PR を失敗させる；P1-11 owner a02 の行 `ci:detection:1`）。main push 時点の検知は記録が無いので不可能であり、文は「次の記録 PR で検知、防止ではない」と書く。悪意ある worker に耐えるべき入力（承認台帳、gate が読む証跡 of record）は GitHub から sha 指定で API 経由（sandbox 内）に読み、ローカルファイルやローカル object database からは読まない。ホスト内の制御（本節 1〜10）は事故と prompt injection による損害の抑制であり、文はそう書く。
111:9. **私設キャッシュ** orchestrator 側の実行は worker と共有するキャッシュ（`~/.cache/uv`、`~/.local/share/uv`、mise）から実行可能物を読まない。`UV_CACHE_DIR` を anchored checkout 配下（`.agents/worklog/uv-cache`、gitignored、worker 書き込み不可）に固定し、`uv run --no-config --no-project --offline --python <信頼ルートの絶対パスのインタプリタ>` で起動する（`--no-config` で uv の設定探索を止め、`--python` の絶対パスで cwd や親の `.venv` の発見を止める；敵対的な `.venv` と `uv.toml` を置いた fixture で検証）。hook は uv を使わず jq と python 標準ライブラリだけで動く。 One trusted interpreter resolver (`scripts/orchestrator/trusted-interpreter.sh`, P1-3 lineage) resolves `<abs>` from the repository's pinned declaration to a regular file under the tool manager's install root (the install root is anchored to the passwd home of the real uid, never to the HOME variable, which a caller can forge), refuses anything else, and every orchestrator-side script consumes it; no script keeps its own `command -v`, `mise which` or PATH fallback (ci-rerun.sh security review 44cb835, 2026-10-19). Shell code of other projects (the agmsg storage layer and its `drivers.partition` sourcing) is never sourced by an orchestrator-side mechanism; counters are read from the agmsg SQLite store read-only with a fixed query or from the pasted record.
113:7. **検証** threat_table は本節の項目 1〜10 に 1 行ずつ対応させる（参照だけでは足りない）。共通テスト補助 `tests/unit/trusted_exec_contract.py`（P1-6 で作る）を使い、仕組みごとに 4 つの red→green テストを持つ：PATH 先頭の shim が実行されない、植え付けた GIT_* と git 設定が効かない、worker ルート配下の信頼パスが拒否される、検証後に差し替えたファイルが消費側に届かない。

     1	#!/usr/bin/env bash
     2	# @file ci-rerun.sh
     3	# @brief Rerun a CI run's failed jobs once, and only when every failure is a known flake of the current head.
     4	# @description
     5	#   Plan 024 P1-3 (T133). Reads the run with `gh run view <run-id> --json`, the log of each job
     6	#   whose conclusion is `failure` (`--log-failed`), `timed_out` or `cancelled` (`--log`), and the
     7	#   signatures of `.github/flakes.yaml` (a list of `{id, job, log_regex, note}`; `job` and
     8	#   `log_regex` are extended regular expressions, matched with `grep -E` against the job name and
     9	#   its log). A job with no log matches nothing. A pull-request run must belong to exactly one
    10	#   pull request whose current head is the run's `headSha`, checked before the logs are read and
    11	#   again right before the rerun: a superseded head's rerun would re-enter `pr-<N>` and cancel the
    12	#   newest head's run. When every such job matches a signature, the run is reserved by an atomic
    13	#   mkdir(2) of `.orchestration/validation/ci-reruns.lock.d/<run-id>` (through python3's os.mkdir), one
    14	#   `<utc>\t<run-id>\t<headSha>\t<job>\t<signature id>` row per job is appended to
    15	#   `.orchestration/validation/ci-reruns.tsv`, and only then `gh run rerun <run-id> --failed`
    16	#   runs. The reservation stays after success, failure or interruption, so a run is rerun at most
    17	#   once, and it blocks only its own run. All of these live in the checkout this script lives in
    18	#   (`git rev-parse --show-toplevel` there).
    19	# @arg $1 int The workflow run id.
    20	# @exitcode 0 Every candidate job matched a signature; the run was reserved and rerun once.
    21	# @exitcode 1 A gh or jq call or the signature file failed (never gh's own codes), or the reserved rerun failed.
    22	# @exitcode 2 Usage error, a run not completed yet, or a run with no failed, timed-out or cancelled job.
    23	# @exitcode 3 A job matched no signature (`real failure: <job>`) or the head is superseded (`superseded: run <id>`).
    24	# @exitcode 4 The run is already reserved or in ci-reruns.tsv; nothing was rerun.
    25	# @example
    26	#   scripts/orchestrator/ci-rerun.sh 39046848147
    27	set -euo pipefail
    28	
    29	if (($# != 1)) || [[ ! $1 =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]]; then
    30	    echo "usage: ci-rerun.sh <run-id>" >&2
    31	    exit 2
    32	fi
    33	run_id=$1
    34	root="$(git -C "$(dirname -- "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" rev-parse --show-toplevel)"
    35	flakes="${root}/.github/flakes.yaml"
    36	ledger="${root}/.orchestration/validation/ci-reruns.tsv"
    37	reservation="${root}/.orchestration/validation/ci-reruns.lock.d/${run_id}"
    38	
    39	if [[ -d ${reservation} ]] ||
    40	    { [[ -f ${ledger} ]] && awk -F '\t' -v id="${run_id}" '$2 == id { found = 1 } END { exit !found }' "${ledger}"; }; then
    41	    echo "already rerun once: run ${run_id} is reserved in ${reservation%/*} or recorded in ${ledger}" >&2
    42	    exit 4
    43	fi
    44	
    45	#
    46	# @description Print each signature of the flake file as `id<TAB>job<TAB>log_regex`, refusing a malformed entry.
    47	# @arg $1 string The flake file.
    48	#
    49	function signatures() {
    50	    local script='import sys, yaml
    51	entries = yaml.safe_load(open(sys.argv[1])) or []
    52	if not isinstance(entries, list):
    53	    sys.exit("ci-rerun: " + sys.argv[1] + " is not a list")
    54	for entry in entries:
    55	    fields = [entry.get(key) for key in ("id", "job", "log_regex")] if isinstance(entry, dict) else [None]
    56	    if not all(isinstance(field, str) and field and "\t" not in field and "\n" not in field for field in fields):
    57	        sys.exit("ci-rerun: malformed signature in " + sys.argv[1] + ": " + repr(entry))
    58	    print("\t".join(fields))'
    59	    # The index form fails inside the Claude sandbox even with PyYAML cached, so the cache goes first.
    60	    uv run --offline --no-project --quiet --with pyyaml python -I -c "${script}" "$1" < /dev/null 2> /dev/null ||
    61	        uv run --no-project --quiet --with pyyaml python -I -c "${script}" "$1" < /dev/null
    62	}
    63	
    64	#
    65	# @description Exit 3 when the run's pull request has moved past its head, 1 when that cannot be read.
    66	#
    67	function require_current_head() {
    68	    local prs current
    69	    [[ ${event} == pull_request ]] || return 0
    70	    prs="$(gh api "repos/{owner}/{repo}/actions/runs/${run_id}" --jq '[.pull_requests[].number | tostring] | join(" ")')" || exit 1
    71	    if [[ ! ${prs} =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]]; then
    72	        echo "ci-rerun: run ${run_id} belongs to no single pull request (${prs:-none})" >&2
    73	        exit 1
    74	    fi
    75	    current="$(gh pr view "${prs}" --json headRefOid --jq .headRefOid)" || exit 1
    76	    [[ ${current} =~ ^[0-9a-f]{40}$ ]] || {
    77	        echo "ci-rerun: pull request ${prs} has no readable head" >&2
    78	        exit 1
    79	    }
    80	    if [[ ${current} != "${head_sha}" ]]; then
    81	        echo "superseded: run ${run_id} ran ${head_sha}, pull request ${prs} is at ${current}"
    82	        exit 3
    83	    fi
    84	}
    85	
    86	# A failed call exits 1: gh's and jq's own codes (gh 4 needs authentication, 2 cancelled) would read as this script's.
    87	run="$(gh run view "${run_id}" --json jobs,status,conclusion,headSha,event)" || exit 1
    88	head_sha="$(jq -r '.headSha // empty' <<< "${run}")" || exit 1
    89	event="$(jq -r '.event // empty' <<< "${run}")" || exit 1
    90	status="$(jq -r '.status // empty' <<< "${run}")" || exit 1
    91	# A run in progress has jobs that are not final yet: --failed would rerun ones no signature checked.
    92	if [[ ${status} != completed ]]; then
    93	    echo "run ${run_id} is not completed (${status:-unknown}); nothing is rerun before it ends" >&2
    94	    exit 2
    95	fi
    96	[[ ${head_sha} =~ ^[0-9a-f]{40}$ ]] || {
    97	    echo "ci-rerun: run ${run_id} has no head sha" >&2
    98	    exit 1
    99	}
   100	candidates="$(jq -r '.jobs[] | select(.conclusion == ("failure", "timed_out", "cancelled"))
   101	    | [.databaseId, .name, .conclusion] | @tsv' <<< "${run}")" || exit 1
   102	if [[ -z ${candidates} ]]; then
   103	    echo "run ${run_id} has no failed, timed-out or cancelled job" >&2
   104	    exit 2
   105	fi
   106	require_current_head
   107	known="$(signatures "${flakes}")" || exit 1
   108	
   109	matched=() real=0
   110	while IFS=$'\t' read -r job_id job conclusion; do
   111	    # --log-failed holds only failed steps; a timed-out or cancelled job's log is read whole.
   112	    option=--log-failed
   113	    [[ ${conclusion} == failure ]] || option=--log
   114	    log="$(gh run view "${run_id}" --job "${job_id}" "${option}")" || exit 1
   115	    signature=""
   116	    while [[ -n ${log} ]] && IFS=$'\t' read -r id job_regex log_regex; do
   117	        if grep -Eq -- "${job_regex}" <<< "${job}" && grep -Eq -- "${log_regex}" <<< "${log}"; then
   118	            signature=${id}
   119	            break
   120	        fi
   121	    done <<< "${known}"
   122	    if [[ -z ${signature} ]]; then
   123	        echo "real failure: ${job}"
   124	        real=1
   125	    else
   126	        matched+=("${job}"$'\t'"${signature}")
   127	    fi
   128	done <<< "${candidates}"
   129	((real == 0)) || exit 3
   130	
   131	# Reserve before rerunning: the directory and the rows outlive a rerun that fails, is refused or is interrupted.
   132	# The claim is mkdir(2) itself: uutils' mkdir (Ubuntu 26.04) returned 0 for a directory another call had just created,
   133	# until uutils bf423f388d (2026-08-28), so the mkdir binary cannot decide which of two concurrent calls wins.
   134	mkdir -p "${reservation%/*}"
   135	claim=0
   136	python3 -I -c 'import os, sys
   137	try:
   138	    os.mkdir(sys.argv[1])
   139	except FileExistsError:
   140	    sys.exit(4)' "${reservation}" 2> /dev/null || claim=$?
   141	if ((claim == 4)); then
   142	    echo "already rerun once: run ${run_id} was reserved by a concurrent call" >&2
   143	    exit 4
   144	elif ((claim != 0)); then
   145	    echo "ci-rerun: cannot create ${reservation}" >&2
   146	    exit 1
   147	fi
   148	now="$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ)"
   149	for row in "${matched[@]}"; do
   150	    printf '%s\t%s\t%s\t%s\n' "${now}" "${run_id}" "${head_sha}" "${row}" >> "${ledger}"
   151	done
   152	require_current_head
   153	if ! gh run rerun "${run_id}" --failed; then
   154	    echo "ci-rerun: gh run rerun failed; run ${run_id} stays reserved in ${reservation%/*}" >&2
   155	    exit 1
   156	fi
   157	printf 'rerun: run %s, %d job(s), recorded in %s\n' "${run_id}" "${#matched[@]}" "${ledger}"

26:- design conclusion (03:5xZ, pending two fact checks): three text rounds show the host is not a security boundary between seats that share one OS user; the regime's hard boundary is GitHub (protected main, required checks, the merge by the orchestrator's identity), and inputs that must resist a malicious worker (the approval ledger, the gate's evidence of record) are read from GitHub at a sha through the API inside the sandbox, never from local files or the local object database; host-local controls target accidental and injected damage and are stated as such. The per-seat mechanism that makes worker-specific policy possible at all is a separate `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR` for worker seats (own settings: no excluded commands, no Herdr socket, minimal trusted hooks with absolute interpreters and `onFailure: block`, Edit denies on trusted paths, failIfUnavailable true); the facts (credentials with a second config dir; onFailure; sandbox per config dir; symlink resolution of file-tool paths) are being verified against the documentation.

Write the failing regressions before the fix and paste red and green output. Every negative security case asserts its specific refusal code, not just exit 2 or an empty marker. Declaration-mutating negative cases (missing, malformed, duplicate, non-exact and metacharacter-bearing declarations, and the exact declaration used for a missing installation) execute the canonical resolver in a temporary copy of the repository tree through the trusted-parent clean prefix. Mutate only that copy's declaration; keep the real uid's passwd-home installation read-only and retain the real passwd-home lookup and install-root anchor. Add `tests/unit/test_trusted_interpreter.py` with class `TrustedInterpreterTest` and these tests:

- `test_pinned_interpreter_ignores_checkout_path_without_mise`: prepend a temporary checkout’s marker-writing `.venv/bin/python3` to PATH with mise unavailable; direct dirty entry exits 2 with `trusted-interpreter: refused: dirty-environment` and no marker. Its positive clean-prefix case invokes the canonical resolver in the real repository, reads that repository’s pinned declaration and the tool-manager-installed interpreter beneath the real uid’s passwd-home install root read-only, and expects exactly one absolute realpath and exit 0. Assert that the printed path is an executable regular file under that install root, then execute it as `"<abs>" -I -c 'import sys; print(sys.version_info[:2])'` and compare the output with the pinned version’s major/minor pair. Assert the resolver exists before considering a skip: its red state is the refusal path, failing by absence before implementation. If the pinned installation is absent, skip only this positive case with the named reason `pinned-installation-unavailable`, including the pin and expected install root, and record the skip in `report.md` and `validation.md`; do not skip dirty-entry or other negative cases. An existing installation that fails validation or version matching is a test failure, not a skip; the marker remains absent in every case.
- `test_missing_install_is_refused`: in the temporary repository copy, declare an exact version whose installation under the real passwd-home root is confirmed absent without creating or modifying it; assert exit 2, `trusted-interpreter: refused: install-missing` and no stdout.
- `test_shell_metacharacter_in_declaration_is_refused`: a copied declaration containing a shell metacharacter exits 2 with `trusted-interpreter: refused: declaration-malformed`, no interpreter stdout and no marker, without executing it.
- `test_duplicate_malformed_nonexact_or_metacharacter_declarations_are_refused`: through the clean prefix in the temporary repository copy, cover duplicate declarations with `trusted-interpreter: refused: declaration-duplicate`; malformed grammar, non-exact versions including `latest`, prefix or suffix lookalikes, and metacharacter-bearing values each assert `trusted-interpreter: refused: declaration-malformed`. Every case exits 2 with no interpreter stdout and no marker.
- `test_missing_or_nonexact_declaration_is_refused`: in the temporary repository copy, an absent declaration asserts `trusted-interpreter: refused: declaration-missing` and `latest` asserts `trusted-interpreter: refused: declaration-malformed`, both with exit 2, no interpreter stdout and no selection of an installed version.
- `test_program_entry_refuses_dirty_environment_or_symlinked_script`: invoke the program with exported functions, `BASH_ENV`/`ENV`, `LD_PRELOAD` and `LD_AUDIT` naming a marker-library path, planted PATH, a forged sanitization sentinel and a symlinked `$0`; dirty-entry cases assert `trusted-interpreter: refused: dirty-environment` and the symlinked-script case asserts `trusted-interpreter: refused: script-path`, each with exit 2, no interpreter stdout and no marker. The trusted-parent clean-prefix success assertion uses the read-only positive case above, with the real repository’s canonical script path and no marker. For direct dirty-entry refusal tests use a non-loadable marker-library path; a loadable library can execute before the script, so test its absence only with the trusted parent's pre-exec clean environment, never as a shell-entry guarantee.
- `test_forged_home_is_refused`: set HOME to a forged directory containing a marker-writing interpreter at the expected mise layout, retaining an otherwise allowed environment; assert exit 2 with `trusted-interpreter: refused: home-mismatch`, no interpreter stdout and no marker before that interpreter can execute. The clean-prefix success assertion with the real uid's passwd home is the read-only positive case above.

`install-symlink` and `install-not-regular` cannot be exercised in CI or by the worker (implemented, refuses by code, verified by reading). Their threat-table row remains `residual`, not `closed`. Do not manufacture symlinked, non-regular, forbidden-root or case-aliased installed candidates under the real passwd home, redirect its install root, or count an earlier refusal as runtime coverage of these checks.

Extend `tests/unit/test_ci_rerun.py::CiRerunTest::test_ci_rerun_pins_its_interpreter_against_a_checkout_and_the_environment`, currently at line 273, with the marker-writing **`python3`** in `.venv/bin` **prepended to PATH**, with mise unavailable. Assert dirty entry exits 2 with `ci-rerun: refused: dirty-environment` and no marker or rerun; then, in a CI-provisioned successful consumer fixture as specified below, use the trusted-parent clean prefix and assert the marker is absent and that the real pinned installation permits exactly one rerun with the pinned interpreter. Also cover safe refusal in a temporary repository copy declaring an exact version whose real passwd-home installation is confirmed absent, asserting the propagated `trusted-interpreter: refused: install-missing` code without modifying that installation.

Retain and adapt `test_ci_rerun_starts_the_pinned_interpreter_by_its_own_path` and `test_ci_rerun_never_runs_a_path_resolved_command_before_the_pin`; their assertions must exercise the new trusted-path contract rather than preserve acceptance of arbitrary PATH wrappers or the removed shims mechanism. Add `test_ci_rerun_executes_as_a_sanitized_program` for exported functions, `BASH_ENV`/`ENV`, `LD_PRELOAD` and `LD_AUDIT` naming a marker-library path, planted PATH, the forged-sentinel case, forged HOME and a worker-owned symlinked `$0`; assert `ci-rerun: refused: dirty-environment` for hostile environment inputs, `ci-rerun: refused: home-mismatch` for forged HOME and `ci-rerun: refused: script-path` for symlinked `$0`, each with exit 2, no marker and no rerun. Apply the same non-loadable-path limitation to direct dirty-loader refusal cases; with a loadable marker library, only the trusted-parent clean-prefix case asserts no marker. Clean-prefix execution in a CI-provisioned successful consumer fixture with the canonical script path and real passwd home must still use the pinned interpreter for both Python invocation sites and permit the expected rerun.

Add these tests to `tests/unit/test_ci_rerun.py::CiRerunTest`. Each destination test starts from an accepted baseline: a temporary copy of the repository tree whose own `scripts/orchestrator/ci-rerun.sh` is executed as the program through the trusted-parent clean prefix. The copy must be the program's confirmed repository root, with its registry, ledger and reservation paths accepted; make one positive assertion of exit 0 and the expected fixture-only behavior first. def egrep(pattern, text):
    """grep -E as the script runs it: 0 a match, 1 none, 2 a pattern that does not compile."""

        command = ["uv", "run", *offline, "--no-project", "--quiet", "--with", "pyyaml", "python", "-I", "-c", script]

## 5.14 信頼ホスト実行契約（trusted-host execution contract、D-024-18）

orchestrator 側の仕組み（merge gate、integrate-merge、orch-gate、dispositions、audit-pool、verdict、chain）は、同じホスト上で同じ UID の worker プロセスと共存する。2026-10-19 の C-80 審査（a05、dispositions-a02、audit-pool-a01 の 3 文、計 19 件の P1）は、どの文も同じ前提を書かず同じ穴を持つことを示した。以後、`security: true` の orchestrator 側タスク文は本節を参照で適用し、本節の各項目に対応する threat 行を持つ。文ごとに言い換えない。

1. **書き込み境界（前提 6、ランタイム別に正直に）** Claude worker 席は専用の設定ディレクトリ `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR=~/.claude-worker`（chezmoi が描画、operator が一度 login）で起動し、orchestrator の `~/.claude` と設定を共有しない。worker 設定：除外コマンド無し、Herdr socket 無し、sandbox の allowWrite は自席 worktree と agmsg store と席別キャッシュだけ、`failIfUnavailable: true`、信頼パス（anchored checkout の `.claude/worktrees/` 以外、`~/.agents`、`~/.claude*`、`~/.local`、`~/.config`）への Edit deny（公式文書：file tool の permission 検査は要求パスと symlink 解決後のパスの両方に deny を適用し、symlink そのものへの書き込みは拒否する）、sandbox の強制範囲はユーザー設定ではなく **managed settings**（`/Library/Application Support/ClaudeCode/managed-settings.json`、公式文書 "Repository settings under an admin-required sandbox"：project/local 設定は sandbox を無効化したり除外・socket を足したりできない）に置く。chezmoi は user ではこのファイルを書けないので、描画した内容を操作者が `sudo` で一度配置する（操作者手順）。worker 設定は worktree 自身の `.claude/**` と `.mcp.json` への Edit deny（`//<anchored>/.claude/worktrees/*/.claude/**`）を持つ（Bash 側は sandbox 既定の settings/hooks の denyWithinAllow）。agmsg store（全席が書く）から配送 hook が driver や plugin を source する上流の経路（storage.sh の `drivers.partition` traversal、`db/trusted-plugins` と `plugins/partition/shared.sh` による差し替え承認）は別タスク（agmsg hardening）の行で、worker 設定は `~/.agents/skills/agmsg/plugins/**` と `~/.agents/skills/agmsg/db/trusted-plugins` への書き込みを deny し（Edit deny は sandbox の deny にも合流する）、hook 側は store 由来の設定値を検証するまで残余と書く。managed settings の配置先は OS で異なる（macOS `/Library/Application Support/ClaudeCode/managed-settings.json`、Linux `/etc/claude-code/managed-settings.json`）。hook は agmsg 配送だけを、hook コマンドを `/usr/bin/env -i PATH=/usr/bin:/bin HOME=<home> …` で始めて（`BASH_ENV` などの継承環境を bash 起動前に消す；PATH 固定だけでは BASH_ENV が先に走る）絶対パスのインタプリタで呼び（上流の agmsg スクリプトは `/usr/bin/env bash` の shebang を持つので、hook 実行環境の PATH が shim を含まないことで束縛する）、各 hook に `onFailure: "block"`。公式文書：linked worktree の session は **main checkout の `.claude/settings.local.json` を読む**ので、worker 向けの hook を worktree の settings.local.json に書いても読まれず、逆に orchestrator の local hooks が worker session でも走る。したがって orchestrator 側の local hooks も guard 経由にし、worker 側の hook は worker config dir の settings に置く。Edit deny の一覧は `git ls-tree` ではなく描画時の静的一覧（manifest に列挙、`.agents/worklog/**` と `.agents/worklog/uv-cache/**` を含む）とし、`~/.agents/**` の全面 deny は agmsg store の書き込みと矛盾するので、`~/.agents/agent-config.yaml`、`~/.agents/skills/**/scripts/**`、`~/.agents/model-profiles.env` などストア以外を列挙する。公式文書で確認した事実（2026-10-19）：`Edit(path)` の deny は Write/MultiEdit/NotebookEdit にも効き、deny → ask → allow の順で最初の一致が決まる；ただし Read/Edit の deny は **Bash sandbox の設定にも合流する**（"Paths and domains from both sandbox settings and permission rules are merged into the final sandbox configuration"）ので、ユーザー設定（全席共有）に `.orchestration/**` の Edit deny を書くと orchestrator の Bash による記録書き込みも止まる。hook は起動時に固定されず、設定ファイルの編集は file watcher が即座に反映する。`sandbox.failIfUnavailable` の既定は fail-open（依存が無ければ sandbox 無しで実行）。したがって Claude 席の境界は Edit deny 規則ではなく、**ユーザーレベルの PreToolUse hook**（`~/.claude/hooks/worker-write-boundary`、chezmoi が描画、`/bin/sh` または絶対パスの `python3 -I`）で作る：cwd の realpath が `<anchored>/.claude/worktrees/` 配下の session では、Write/Edit/MultiEdit/NotebookEdit の対象が cwd の外、または `<cwd>/.claude/**`・`<cwd>/.mcp.json` のとき `permissionDecision: deny` を返す。Bash 側は sandbox の既定 `denyWithinAllow`（settings、hooks）と `allowWrite`（自席 worktree）が担う。`failIfUnavailable: true` を全席に置く。 Codex 席は sandbox（workspace-write）がツールの書き込み全部を自席の worktree と列挙パスに限る。Claude 席は Seatbelt sandbox が Bash を限るだけで、Write/Edit/MultiEdit/NotebookEdit、hook、MCP プロセスは sandbox 外で動き、permission policy（permgate の決定論ポリシー）が path で拒否しない限り anchored checkout や `~/.agents` にも書ける（2026-10-19 時点、描画済み設定にその deny は無い）。したがって前提 6 は「Codex 席は成立、Claude 席は P1-15 が描画設定に Edit の path deny を入れるまで残余」と書く。P1-15 以前に「worker は信頼ルートに書けない」と書く文は誤りで、C-80 が拒否する。P1-15 が入るまで、承認の出所は項目 8 の「コミット済み台帳」で守る。書き込みは自席の worktree（`.claude/worktrees/<name>`）、scratch、列挙されたツールキャッシュに限る。anchored checkout（`.claude/worktrees/` 以外）、`~/.agents/agent-config.yaml`、`~/.claude/hooks`、信頼ルートのバイナリ、orchestrator の証跡・スナップショット・lock は worker から書けない。render テストが描画後の deny を検証する。残余は「どの席 sandbox にも属さない同一 UID プロセス」と、Worker Playbook step 4 が sandbox 外に出す少数のコマンド（gh）。後者は D-024-17（P1-15）で絶対パスの実行体に束縛する。
2. **無害化した環境を最初に** 最初の処理として `env -i` に入り、PATH は信頼ルートで解決した絶対バイナリへのリンクだけの私設ディレクトリ（shims、空、相対は拒否）。git は毎回 `GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM=1 GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL=/dev/null -c core.hooksPath=/dev/null -c core.fsmonitor=false -c protocol.allow=never -c protocol.https.allow=always` と明示 `-C`（https だけ許す。`protocol.allow=never` 単独は必要な fetch を止める）。 検査の読み取りは `GIT_NO_LAZY_FETCH=1` で部分クローンの遅延 fetch（checkout 設定の credential.helper を起動しうる）を止め、`-c submodule.recurse=false --ignore-submodules=all` で submodule 内の status/diff（submodule 自身の設定が filter を有効化しうる）を止める。`.gitmodules` を持つ checkout は検査対象として拒否する。 `-C` は作業木を束縛しないので（repo-local の `core.worktree` が読み先を checkout 外へ向けられる）、git は `--git-dir=<checkout>/.git --work-tree=<checkout>` を明示し、`git rev-parse --show-toplevel` の realpath が checkout と一致しないときは拒否する。 premise や検査の「コマンド実行」は、固定語彙の読み取り専用プローブ（`git rev-parse <ref>`、`test -e <path>`、`grep -c <pattern> <file>` のように検査器自身が解釈する形）だけを許し、任意のシェル文字列は実行しない（本文のハッシュはコマンド文を認証するだけで、`python -I scripts/x.py` のファイル被演算子や `source ./x.sh` の中身は認証しない）。diff と show は `--no-ext-diff --no-textconv` と `-c core.attributesFile=/dev/null -c core.sshCommand=false` を付け、PR 側の `.gitattributes`（external diff、textconv、filter）が検査内容や実行物を変えないようにする。Python は `-I`。PATH 探索で実行するものは無い。 A shell script that runs under a PR-controlled or worker-controlled environment (verdict.sh, the chain glue, CI steps) is executed as a program with `#!/bin/bash -p`, never as `bash <file>`, and only by a trusted parent that supplies the clean environment before the exec: `/usr/bin/env -i PATH=/usr/bin:/bin HOME=<passwd home> LANG=C.UTF-8 /bin/bash --noprofile --norc -p <absolute script path> …` (long options before `-p`). Privileged mode discards inherited functions, `BASH_ENV`/`ENV`, `SHELLOPTS`, `BASHOPTS`, `CDPATH` and `GLOBIGNORE`, but on Linux an inherited `LD_PRELOAD`/`LD_AUDIT` already executes while the loader maps `/bin/bash`, before `-p` or any statement runs, so a self re-exec inside the script is not a boundary and re-executing `"$0"` would reopen the caller's pathname (a worker-owned symlink can be swapped between the two executions); the program therefore verifies and refuses instead of repairing: its environment must consist of exactly the allowed names (checked through absolute paths `/usr/bin/env`, `/usr/bin/grep`), `$0` resolved with `realpath` must be a regular file under the repository root, and HOME must equal the passwd home of the real uid (`/usr/bin/dscl . -read /Users/<user> NFSHomeDirectory` on macOS, `/usr/bin/getent passwd <uid>` on Linux), otherwise exit 2 with a named message. A caller-set sentinel variable is not a sanitization proof, because the caller controls it (ci-efficiency a04 and verdict a04 C-80 reviews, 2026-10-19). No script accepts a caller-supplied executable directory; interpreters are the fixed absolute paths item 9 names.
3. **信頼パスの境界検査** 仕組みが信頼するパス（スクリプト、台帳、証跡、worktree、lock、出力先）は realpath で解決し、`.claude/worktrees/` または manifest の worker ルート配下なら拒否（ask/hold）。symlink 構成要素は解決後に比較し、大小文字を区別しないファイルシステムでは同一性比較を大小文字非依存で行う。 A validator or scanner enumerates its inputs by walking the filesystem under fixed roots (every regular file, ignored or untracked included), never by asking git which files exist: `git ls-files` trusts the index and the working-tree `.gitignore` rules, which a same-UID writer edits without a commit (a07 C-80 rounds 1 to 3, 2026-10-19); git is consulted only to confirm the root (`--show-toplevel` equality with a real `.git` directory) and never as an exclusion authority.
4. **検証したものを実行し、検証した内容を渡す** 検証した実行体はその realpath で実行する（再探索しない）。検証したファイル内容は信頼ルート配下のスナップショット（0600、一意名）として渡し、使用直前に sha256 を再照合する。判断に読んだ証跡は sha256 を出力に記録する。
5. **出力と予約の同一性** 名前には 40 桁の head を使う（7 桁で衝突させない）。lock は信頼ルート配下の固定 inode に O_EXCL で作り、置換を inode 比較で検出する。物理スロット 1 つに lock 1 つ。
6. **正直な残余** 境界だけが緩和するリスクを `closed` と書かない。各文は残余を 1 行で明記する。
0. **脅威モデル（D-024-21）** 席は同じ OS ユーザで動き、ホストは席間の信頼境界ではない（同一 UID のプロセスは symlink 差し替え、共有 `.git` の object 差し替え、hook の子プロセス、Herdr socket 経由の host 実行で、ホスト内のどの検査も迂回できる）。regime の硬い境界は GitHub（保護された main、必須チェック、orchestrator の identity によるマージ）。ただしこの境界は **worker 席が orchestrator の GitHub 資格情報を共有しない**ときだけ成立する（D-024-22：worker 席は専用の GitHub identity（別アカウントまたは App のトークン）で `gh` に login し、ruleset の bypass 一覧に入らない。orchestrator の keychain トークンを worker の config dir に持ち込まない。identity が分かれるまで「GitHub が境界」は未成立と書く）。**同一 macOS ユーザでは資格情報は分離できない**（gh は keychain の項目を host/user で引き、同じ UID の gh は orchestrator のトークンを読める。`GH_CONFIG_DIR` は設定を分けるだけ）。防止には worker 席を別の OS ユーザ（または別ホスト）で動かすしかなく、それは D-024-23 の将来項目（P1-16、操作者の判断）。それまで GitHub 境界は **検知**で守る：記録は boundary の記録 PR まで main に無いので、検知は次の記録 PR の regime ジョブで行う（`.orchestration/acceptance/**` に触れる PR で、前回 boundary 以降の main の各マージ commit に、gate 通過の証跡を持つ受け入れ記録が対応することを検査し、無ければその PR を失敗させる；P1-11 owner a02 の行 `ci:detection:1`）。main push 時点の検知は記録が無いので不可能であり、文は「次の記録 PR で検知、防止ではない」と書く。悪意ある worker に耐えるべき入力（承認台帳、gate が読む証跡 of record）は GitHub から sha 指定で API 経由（sandbox 内）に読み、ローカルファイルやローカル object database からは読まない。ホスト内の制御（本節 1〜10）は事故と prompt injection による損害の抑制であり、文はそう書く。
8. **承認はコミット済み台帳から読む** permission の決定行（`scope=permission approved_by=user diff_sha256=…`）は anchored checkout の作業コピーではなく、コミット済みの台帳から読む。読み方は `git ls-remote origin refs/heads/main` が返す commit sha を取り、GitHub API（`gh api repos/<owner>/<repo>/contents/.orchestration/decisions.tsv?ref=<sha>`、sandbox 内）でその sha の内容を取得する。ローカルの `git show` は共有 object database が書けるため中間 tree の差し替えで偽物を返しうる（第 3 回審査の所見 2）（ローカルの ref 名 `origin/main` は経由しない。`refs/replace/` も書けるので replacement を無効にする。Codex worker は `.git/objects` と `.git/refs` に書けるので ref は差し替えられるが、sha で指した object は内容アドレスで偽造できない）。作業コピーの行は提案で、記録 PR が main にマージされて初めて承認になる（PR の承認は操作者の操作）。偽造には main へのマージが要る。 The same applies to the PR content a mechanism evaluates (the verdict, the audit precheck, the counters): the changeset is read from GitHub at the 40-hex head (`gh api repos/{owner}/{repo}/compare/<base40>...<head40>`, `…/contents/<path>?ref=<head40>`) or from a private clone whose object store no worker can write; the main checkout's shared `.git/objects` and its `origin/main` ref are not evidence sources, because Codex workers write them (a04 C-80 round 1, 2026-10-19). Evidence files are named and matched by the full 40-hex head, never by a 7-character prefix. Every `gh` invocation names the repository explicitly: `gh pr`, `gh run` and `gh issue` subcommands take `--repo mryfmo/dotfiles` or the full pull-request URL, and `gh api` (which has no `--repo` flag) takes the explicit `repos/mryfmo/dotfiles/…` endpoint path, never a `{owner}/{repo}` placeholder: the local repository configuration selects the default repository otherwise, and a matching head sha does not bind the repository because a fork can hold the same commit (verdict a04 C-80 round 2, 2026-10-19). A mechanism whose application path runs code this contract does not yet cover (the agmsg storage layer sourced by `dispatch.sh`/`history.sh`; the integration gate until its own hardening task lands) names that task as the prerequisite of its `--apply` mode and runs shadow-only until the prerequisite is merged.
9. **私設キャッシュ** orchestrator 側の実行は worker と共有するキャッシュ（`~/.cache/uv`、`~/.local/share/uv`、mise）から実行可能物を読まない。`UV_CACHE_DIR` を anchored checkout 配下（`.agents/worklog/uv-cache`、gitignored、worker 書き込み不可）に固定し、`uv run --no-config --no-project --offline --python <信頼ルートの絶対パスのインタプリタ>` で起動する（`--no-config` で uv の設定探索を止め、`--python` の絶対パスで cwd や親の `.venv` の発見を止める；敵対的な `.venv` と `uv.toml` を置いた fixture で検証）。hook は uv を使わず jq と python 標準ライブラリだけで動く。 One trusted interpreter resolver (`scripts/orchestrator/trusted-interpreter.sh`, P1-3 lineage) resolves `<abs>` from the repository's pinned declaration to a regular file under the tool manager's install root (the install root is anchored to the passwd home of the real uid, never to the HOME variable, which a caller can forge), refuses anything else, and every orchestrator-side script consumes it; no script keeps its own `command -v`, `mise which` or PATH fallback (ci-rerun.sh security review 44cb835, 2026-10-19). Shell code of other projects (the agmsg storage layer and its `drivers.partition` sourcing) is never sourced by an orchestrator-side mechanism; counters are read from the agmsg SQLite store read-only with a fixed query or from the pasted record.
10. **worker は sandbox 外でコマンドを実行しない（D-024-17 の改訂）** 2026-10-19T03:45Z の実測で `gh api` は Claude sandbox の中から成功した（sandbox の proxy は CONNECT 型で TLS を終端しない；keychain のトークンも読めた）。worker 席の除外コマンドは空にし、gh も git push も sandbox 内の proxy 経由で実行する。Codex 席は `sandbox_workspace_write.network_access = true` で同じ状態にし、permgate の codex ポリシーは sandbox 外への escape を承認しない（permgate の変更は操作者の手順）。wrapper 方式（絶対パス、env -i、固定 GH_CONFIG_DIR）は LD_PRELOAD と引数転送（`gh repo clone -- --config`）を閉じられないので採らない。
7. **検証** threat_table は本節の項目 1〜10 に 1 行ずつ対応させる（参照だけでは足りない）。共通テスト補助 `tests/unit/trusted_exec_contract.py`（P1-6 で作る）を使い、仕組みごとに 4 つの red→green テストを持つ：PATH 先頭の shim が実行されない、植え付けた GIT_* と git 設定が効かない、worker ルート配下の信頼パスが拒否される、検証後に差し替えたファイルが消費側に届かない。

The controls distinguish execution binding from the acknowledged same-user, shared-capability, agmsg sourcing and path-race residuals. Enforcement claims match the documented [managed sandbox scope](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/sandboxing#repository-settings-under-an-admin-required-sandbox) and [symlink checks](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/permissions#symlinks).

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# @file ci-rerun.sh
# @brief Rerun a CI run's failed jobs once, and only when every failure is a known flake of the current head.
# @description
#   Plan 024 P1-3 (T133). Reads the run with `gh run view <run-id> --json`, the log of each job
#   whose conclusion is `failure` (`--log-failed`), `timed_out` or `cancelled` (`--log`), and the
#   signatures of `.github/flakes.yaml` (a list of `{id, job, log_regex, note}`; `job` and
#   `log_regex` are extended regular expressions, matched with `grep -E` against the job name and
#   its log). A job with no log matches nothing. A pull-request run must belong to exactly one
#   pull request whose current head is the run's `headSha`, checked before the logs are read and
#   again right before the rerun: a superseded head's rerun would re-enter `pr-<N>` and cancel the
#   newest head's run. When every such job matches a signature, the run is reserved by an atomic
#   mkdir(2) of `.orchestration/validation/ci-reruns.lock.d/<run-id>` (through python3's os.mkdir), one
#   `<utc>\t<run-id>\t<headSha>\t<job>\t<signature id>` row per job is appended to
#   `.orchestration/validation/ci-reruns.tsv`, and only then `gh run rerun <run-id> --failed`
#   runs. The reservation stays after success, failure or interruption, so a run is rerun at most
#   once, and it blocks only its own run. All of these live in the checkout this script lives in
#   (`git rev-parse --show-toplevel` there). Before reading anything, uv, python3, gh, jq, git and mkdir are resolved
#   as `mise which` answers them under env -i from / (else `command -v`; a shims or relative path exits 1); all but
#   python3 are linked into a private directory and PATH becomes those links then /usr/bin:/bin. python3 is always
#   started by its own path, never a link (CPython reads a pyvenv.cfg beside and above the path it starts by), as
#   `python3 -I` and as uv's `--python` for `uv run --no-config --no-project` under env -i from the root, so no
#   checkout's `.python-version`, `.venv` or executable and no inherited UV_*, VIRTUAL_ENV or PYTHON* variable picks
#   the interpreter (security:4fe2438:1). Before PATH is pinned, every other command (mktemp, env, ln and the EXIT trap's
#   rm) runs by its absolute system path; mise, the resolver itself, is the one program found through PATH.
# @arg $1 int The workflow run id.
# @exitcode 0 Every candidate job matched a signature; the run was reserved and rerun once.
# @exitcode 1 A tool resolves only to a shims or relative path, a gh or jq call or the signature file failed (never
#   gh's own codes), or the reserved rerun failed.
# @exitcode 2 Usage error, a run not completed yet, or a run with no failed, timed-out or cancelled job.
# @exitcode 3 A job matched no signature (`real failure: <job>`) or the head is superseded (`superseded: run <id>`).
# @exitcode 4 The run is already reserved or in ci-reruns.tsv; nothing was rerun.
# @example
#   scripts/orchestrator/ci-rerun.sh 39046848147
set -euo pipefail

if (($# != 1)) || [[ ! $1 =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]]; then
    echo "usage: ci-rerun.sh <run-id>" >&2
    exit 2
fi
run_id=$1
bin="$(/usr/bin/mktemp -d "${TMPDIR:-/tmp}/ci-rerun-bin.XXXXXX")" # system paths: PATH is not pinned yet
trap '/bin/rm -rf -- "${bin}"' EXIT
for tool in python3 uv gh jq git mkdir; do
    found="$(cd / && /usr/bin/env -i PATH="${PATH}" HOME="${HOME}" mise which "${tool}" 2> /dev/null || command -v "${tool}")" || found=""
    # The directory is judged physically, so a PATH entry that links to a shims directory is refused too.
    if ! { [[ ${found} == /* ]] && where="$(cd -P -- "${found%/*}" 2> /dev/null && pwd -P)" && [[ ${where}/ != */shims/* ]]; }; then
        echo "ci-rerun: no ${tool} outside a shims directory ('${found}')" >&2
        exit 1
    fi
    if [[ ${tool} == python3 ]]; then
        py=${found}
    else
        /bin/ln -s -- "${found}" "${bin}/${tool}"
    fi
done
PATH="${bin}:/usr/bin:/bin"
root="$(git -C "$(dirname -- "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" rev-parse --show-toplevel)"
flakes="${root}/.github/flakes.yaml"
ledger="${root}/.orchestration/validation/ci-reruns.tsv"
reservation="${root}/.orchestration/validation/ci-reruns.lock.d/${run_id}"

if [[ -d ${reservation} ]] ||
    { [[ -f ${ledger} ]] && awk -F '\t' -v id="${run_id}" '$2 == id { found = 1 } END { exit !found }' "${ledger}"; }; then
    echo "already rerun once: run ${run_id} is reserved in ${reservation%/*} or recorded in ${ledger}" >&2
    exit 4
fi

#
# @description Print each signature of the flake file as `id<TAB>job<TAB>log_regex`, refusing a malformed entry.
# @arg $1 string The flake file.
#
function signatures() {
    local script='import sys, yaml
entries = yaml.safe_load(open(sys.argv[1])) or []
if not isinstance(entries, list):
    sys.exit("ci-rerun: " + sys.argv[1] + " is not a list")
for entry in entries:
    fields = [entry.get(key) for key in ("id", "job", "log_regex")] if isinstance(entry, dict) else [None]
    if not all(isinstance(field, str) and field and "\t" not in field and "\n" not in field for field in fields):
        sys.exit("ci-rerun: malformed signature in " + sys.argv[1] + ": " + repr(entry))
    print("\t".join(fields))'
    local uvr=(env -i PATH="${PATH}" HOME="${HOME}" UV_CACHE_DIR="${HOME}/.cache/uv" uv run --no-config --no-project
        --quiet --python "${py}" --with pyyaml)
    # The index form fails inside the Claude sandbox even with PyYAML cached, so the cache goes first.
    (cd -- "${root}" && "${uvr[@]}" --offline python -I -c "${script}" "$1" < /dev/null 2> /dev/null) ||
        (cd -- "${root}" && "${uvr[@]}" python -I -c "${script}" "$1" < /dev/null)
}

#
# @description Exit 3 when the run's pull request has moved past its head, 1 when that cannot be read.
#
function require_current_head() {
    local prs current
    [[ ${event} == pull_request ]] || return 0
    prs="$(gh api "repos/{owner}/{repo}/actions/runs/${run_id}" --jq '[.pull_requests[].number | tostring] | join(" ")')" || exit 1
    if [[ ! ${prs} =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]]; then
        echo "ci-rerun: run ${run_id} belongs to no single pull request (${prs:-none})" >&2
        exit 1
    fi
    current="$(gh pr view "${prs}" --json headRefOid --jq .headRefOid)" || exit 1
    [[ ${current} =~ ^[0-9a-f]{40}$ ]] || {
        echo "ci-rerun: pull request ${prs} has no readable head" >&2
        exit 1
    }
    if [[ ${current} != "${head_sha}" ]]; then
        echo "superseded: run ${run_id} ran ${head_sha}, pull request ${prs} is at ${current}"
        exit 3
    fi
}

# A failed call exits 1: gh's and jq's own codes (gh 4 needs authentication, 2 cancelled) would read as this script's.
run="$(gh run view "${run_id}" --json jobs,status,conclusion,headSha,event)" || exit 1
head_sha="$(jq -r '.headSha // empty' <<< "${run}")" || exit 1
event="$(jq -r '.event // empty' <<< "${run}")" || exit 1
status="$(jq -r '.status // empty' <<< "${run}")" || exit 1
# A run in progress has jobs that are not final yet: --failed would rerun ones no signature checked.
if [[ ${status} != completed ]]; then
    echo "run ${run_id} is not completed (${status:-unknown}); nothing is rerun before it ends" >&2
    exit 2
fi
[[ ${head_sha} =~ ^[0-9a-f]{40}$ ]] || {
    echo "ci-rerun: run ${run_id} has no head sha" >&2
    exit 1
}
candidates="$(jq -r '.jobs[] | select(.conclusion == ("failure", "timed_out", "cancelled"))
    | [.databaseId, .name, .conclusion] | @tsv' <<< "${run}")" || exit 1
if [[ -z ${candidates} ]]; then
    echo "run ${run_id} has no failed, timed-out or cancelled job" >&2
    exit 2
fi
require_current_head
known="$(signatures "${flakes}")" || exit 1

matched=() real=0
while IFS=$'\t' read -r job_id job conclusion; do
    # --log-failed holds only failed steps; a timed-out or cancelled job's log is read whole.
    option=--log-failed
    [[ ${conclusion} == failure ]] || option=--log
    log="$(gh run view "${run_id}" --job "${job_id}" "${option}")" || exit 1
    signature=""
    while [[ -n ${log} ]] && IFS=$'\t' read -r id job_regex log_regex; do
        if grep -Eq -- "${job_regex}" <<< "${job}" && grep -Eq -- "${log_regex}" <<< "${log}"; then
            signature=${id}
            break
        fi
    done <<< "${known}"
    if [[ -z ${signature} ]]; then
        echo "real failure: ${job}"
        real=1
    else
        matched+=("${job}"$'\t'"${signature}")
    fi
done <<< "${candidates}"
((real == 0)) || exit 3

# Reserve before rerunning: the directory and the rows outlive a rerun that fails, is refused or is interrupted.
# The claim is mkdir(2) itself: uutils' mkdir (Ubuntu 26.04) returned 0 for a directory another call had just created,
# until uutils bf423f388d (2026-08-28), so the mkdir binary cannot decide which of two concurrent calls wins.
mkdir -p "${reservation%/*}"
claim=0
"${py}" -I -c 'import os, sys
try:
    os.mkdir(sys.argv[1])
except FileExistsError:
    sys.exit(4)' "${reservation}" 2> /dev/null || claim=$?
if ((claim == 4)); then
    echo "already rerun once: run ${run_id} was reserved by a concurrent call" >&2
    exit 4
elif ((claim != 0)); then
    echo "ci-rerun: cannot create ${reservation}" >&2
    exit 1
fi
now="$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ)"
for row in "${matched[@]}"; do
    printf '%s\t%s\t%s\t%s\n' "${now}" "${run_id}" "${head_sha}" "${row}" >> "${ledger}"
done
require_current_head
if ! gh run rerun "${run_id}" --failed; then
    echo "ci-rerun: gh run rerun failed; run ${run_id} stays reserved in ${reservation%/*}" >&2
    exit 1
fi
printf 'rerun: run %s, %d job(s), recorded in %s\n' "${run_id}" "${#matched[@]}" "${ledger}"

.orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-ci-efficiency-followup-a04.md:127:Before reading or writing the flake registry, ledger or reservation state, resolve `.github/flakes.yaml`, `.orchestration/validation/ci-reruns.tsv` and the reservation parent `.orchestration/validation/ci-reruns.lock.d` with `realpath`. Require the registry and ledger to be regular files and the reservation parent to be a directory under the confirmed repository root, outside manifest worker roots, worker-writable caches and `$TMPDIR`. Refuse symlinks in the destination or any path component below that root, and, on a case-insensitive filesystem, a destination differing from its declared pathname only by case. Use only the validated canonical destinations for subsequent reads and effects. Apply the same checks to an existing per-run reservation directory before inspecting its claim and to a newly claimed directory before using it. Missing output objects may be created only at the existing effect point after validating their existing parent chain and resolving the intended destination; resolve and type-check each newly created object before appending ledger data or using a claim. Preserve atomic per-run creation and never replace an existing object during validation. Any destination-validation failure exits 2 with exactly one `ci-rerun: refused: <code>` line before registry parsing, ledger access or a rerun, leaving redirected targets unchanged. Destination codes are `destination-outside-root` for a resolved path outside the confirmed repository or in a forbidden root, `destination-symlink` for an otherwise contained path with a symlink in the destination or its parent chain, `destination-case-alias` for an otherwise contained case alias, `destination-not-regular` for an invalid registry or ledger file type, and `destination-not-directory` for an invalid reservation directory type.         exclude:

        # Non-required canary for the next Ubuntu image: it shows how the suite

    runs-on: ${{ matrix.os }}
    continue-on-error: ${{ matrix.os == 'ubuntu-26.04' }}
    env:
      # Export matrix values to shell scripts so existing test helpers can use
      # simple `OS`/`SYSTEM` checks without depending on GitHub expression syntax.
      OS: ${{ matrix.os }}
      SYSTEM: ${{ matrix.system }}
      # Keep Codecov naming deterministic per job. This makes it easy to trace
      # upload sessions in Codecov API/UI and avoids accidental session overlap.
      CODECOV_FLAGS: ${{ matrix.os }}-${{ matrix.system }}
      CODECOV_NAME: codecov-dotfiles-${{ matrix.os }}-${{ matrix.system }}
      GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}

    steps:
      - name: Configure Git defaults
        run: git config --global init.defaultBranch main

      - name: Checkout repository
        uses: actions/checkout@3d3c42e5aac5ba805825da76410c181273ba90b1 # v7
        with:
          fetch-depth: 0
          persist-credentials: false

      - name: Decide whether the diff needs the unit tests
        id: decide
        env:
          EVENT_NAME: ${{ github.event_name }}
          BASE_REF: ${{ github.base_ref }}
          BEFORE_SHA: ${{ github.event.before }}
          HEAD_SHA: ${{ github.sha }}
        run: |
          set -euo pipefail

          # Decided in this job, never in a job it needs: a job skipped because
          # a needed job failed reports Success even as a required check, so a
          # failed decision has to fail this job instead.
          if [ "${EVENT_NAME}" = "pull_request" ]; then
            # A full fetch: a shallow one cuts the history of a base tip that
            # moved after the checkout, and the diff then has no merge base.
            git fetch --no-tags origin "${BASE_REF}"
            diff_range="origin/${BASE_REF}...${HEAD_SHA}"
          elif [ -n "${BEFORE_SHA}" ] && [ "${BEFORE_SHA}" != "0000000000000000000000000000000000000000" ]; then
            diff_range="${BEFORE_SHA}...${HEAD_SHA}"
          else
            diff_range="${HEAD_SHA}^...${HEAD_SHA}"

    def reservation(self, run_id):
        return self.ledger.with_name("ci-reruns.lock.d") / run_id

    def reset(self):
        self.ledger.unlink(missing_ok=True)
        shutil.rmtree(self.ledger.with_name("ci-reruns.lock.d"), ignore_errors=True)
        self.log.write_text("")
        for turn in self.t.glob("gh.log.*"):
            turn.unlink()
        (self.t / "runs.json").write_text(json.dumps(self.runs))

    def start(self, run_id, **env):
        return subprocess.Popen(
            ["bash", str(self.repo / "scripts/orchestrator/ci-rerun.sh"), run_id],
            env=os.environ
            | {
                "PATH": str(self.t / "bin") + os.pathsep + os.environ["PATH"],
                "GH_LOG": str(self.log),
                "GH_RUNS": str(self.t / "runs.json"),
                "GH_REPO_ROOT": str(self.repo),
                **env,
            },
            stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
            stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
            stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
            text=True,
        )

    def finish(self, process):
        out, err = process.communicate(timeout=120)
        return subprocess.CompletedProcess(process.args, process.returncode, out, err)
